, there exists a pullback diagram

\textstyle{E_{i}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{\tilde{Z}_{i}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{E_{i-1}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{Z_{i}}

where
\tilde{Z}_{i}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>Z_{i}
is a generically finite dominant map with
\dim(Z_{i})\leq d
.

Example 4.2
.

Consider the space
\mathbb{A}^{n}_{\mathbf{a}}
of monic degree
n
-polynomials. This has a canonical
n
-sheeted branched cover
E_{P_{n}}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>\mathbb{A}^{n}_{\mathbf{a}}
where
E_{P_{n}}
is the space of monic degree
n
polynomials with a choice of root, and the map forgets the root. By definition

\RD(n):=\RD(E_{P_{n}}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>\mathbb{A}^{n}_{\mathbf{a}}).

We now extend the notion of resolvent degree to general dominant maps. We adopt the following convention to avoid pathologies.

Convention 4.3
.

By a dominant map, we mean a map
Y\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X
that is both dominant, and is such that every irreducible component of
Y
maps dominantly onto some irreducible component of
X
.

Definition 4.4

(
Rational multi-section).

Let
Y\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.45647pt\raise 4.50694pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.50694pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\pi}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 6.79462pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 19.58923pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X
be a dominant map of
K
-varieties. A
rational multi-section
is a subvariety

U\subset Y
such that the restriction
{\pi|_{U}}:U\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X
is a generically finite dominant map.

Lemma 4.5
.

Every dominant map
Y\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X
admits a dense set of rational multi-sections, i.e. the closure of their union is all of
Y
.

Proof.

First assume that
X
is irreducible. Let
\overline{K(X)}
be an algebraic closure of the rational functions of
X
. Then every point of
Y(\overline{K(X)})
is a germ of a rational multi-section, and, by Hilbert’s Nullstellensatz, the closure of the union of all of these contains the generic fiber of
Y\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X
; in particular it is dense. For the general case, the argument above exhibits a dense set of rational multi-sections over each irreducible component. Their union gives a dense set of rational multi-sections of
Y\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X
.
∎

It will be useful to extend the definition of resolvent degree from generically finite dominant rational maps to all dominant rational maps.

Definition 4.6

(
Resolvent degree of a dominant map).

Let
Y\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.45647pt\raise 4.50694pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.50694pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\pi}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 6.79462pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 19.58923pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X
be a dominant map of
K
-varieties. The
resolvent degree of the dominant map
,
\RD(Y\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X)
is defined to be the minimum
d
for which there exists a dense set of rational multi-sections
\{U_{\alpha}\subset Y\}
with
\RD(U_{\alpha}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X)\leq d
for all
\alpha
.

We will need a few basic facts about the resolvent degree of a dominant map.

Lemma 4.7
.

Let
Y\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X
be a dominant map of
K
-varieties.

1.

\RD(Y\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X)\leq\dim(X)
.

2.

Let
Z\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X
be any dominant map of
K
-varieties. Then

\RD(Y\times_{X}Z\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>Z)\leq\RD(Y\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X).

3.

If
Y\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X
is birationally equivalent to
W\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>Z
, then

\RD(Y\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X)=\RD(W\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>Z).

4.

If
X=\bigcup X_{i}
is a union of irreducible components, write
\{Y_{i,j}\}
for the set of irreducible components of
Y
which dominate
X_{i}
. Then

\RD(Y\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X)=\max_{i,j}\{\RD(Y_{i,j}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X_{i})\}.

Proof.

These follow immediately from the definition and the analogous properties for resolvent degree of generically finite dominant maps (cf.
[
FW19
, Lemmas 2.5, 2.6]
).
∎

Lemma 4.8
.

Let
Y\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X
be a surjective map (on geometric points). Let
Z\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X
be any map. Then

\RD(Y|_{Z}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>Z)\leq\dim(X).

Proof.

Let
W\subset X
be the Zariski closure of the image of
Z\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X
. By construction, the map
Z\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>W
is dominant. The surjectivity of
Y\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X
implies that the restriction

Y|_{W}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>W

is dominant. Therefore, by Lemma
4.7
,

\displaystyle\RD(Y|_{Z}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>Z)

\displaystyle\leq\RD(Y|_{W}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>W)

\displaystyle\leq\dim(W)

\displaystyle\leq\dim(X).

∎

Lemma 4.9
.

Let
Y\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X
be a generically finite dominant map. Then Definition
4.6
specializes to Definition
4.1
for
Y\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X
, i.e. they give equivalent notions of resolvent degree.

Proof.

By Lemma
4.7

4
and
[
FW19
, Lemma 2.6]
, it suffices to prove this when
Y
is irreducible. In this case, any rational multi-section
U\subset Y
of
Y\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X
must be dense in
Y
. In particular, it must be birational to
Y
. From the birational invariance of
\RD
for generically finite dominant maps, we conclude that
\RD(U\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X)=\RD(Y\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X)
(as generically finite dominant maps). The lemma follows.
∎

Lemma 4.10
.

Let
Z\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.45398pt\raise 5.00694pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.00694pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\pi_{1}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 8.74355pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 23.48709pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>Y\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.45398pt\raise 5.00694pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.00694pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\pi_{2}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 8.74355pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 23.48709pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X
be a pair of dominant maps of
K
-varieties. Then

\displaystyle\RD(Z\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X)

\displaystyle\geq\RD(Y\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X)

and

\displaystyle\RD(Z\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X)

\displaystyle\leq\max\{\RD(Z\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>Y),\RD(Y\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X)\}.

with equality when either
Z\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>Y
or
Y\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X
is generically finite.

Proof.

For the first inequality, let
\{U_{\alpha}\subset Z\}
be a dense set of rational multi-sections of
Z\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X
with
\RD(U_{\alpha}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X)\leq d
for all
\alpha
. Then, shrinking each
U_{\alpha}
as necessary (e.g. restricting to the preimage in
U
of an affine open in

), its (scheme theoretic) image
V_{\alpha}:=\Image(U_{\alpha}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>Y)
is a subscheme of
Y
, and thus a rational multi-section of
Y\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X
. Since
Z\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>Y
is dominant, that
\{U_{\alpha}\subset Z\}
is dense implies that
\{V_{\alpha}\subset Y\}
is dense. By
[
FW19
, Lemma 2.7]
, we conclude that
\RD(U_{\alpha}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X)\geq\RD(V_{\alpha}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X)
. Minimizing over all
\{U_{\alpha}\subset Z\}
, we conclude that

\RD(Z\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X)\geq\RD(Y\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X).

For the second inequality, let
\{U_{\alpha}\subset Z\}
be a dense set of rational multi-sections for
Z\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>Y
and
\{V_{\beta}\subset Y\}
a dense set of rational multi-sections for
Y\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X
. Then

\{W_{\alpha,\beta}:=U_{\alpha}\times_{Y}V_{\beta}\subset Z\}

is a dense set of rational multi-sections for
Z\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X
. By
[
FW19
, Lemmas 2.5, 2.7]
,

\RD(W_{\alpha,\beta}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X)\leq\max\{\RD(U_{\alpha}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>Y),\RD(V_{\beta}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X)\}.

Minimizing over all such collections
\{U_{\alpha}\},\{V_{\beta}\}
, we conclude

\RD(Z\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X)\}.

To show the equalities when
\dim(Y)=\dim(X)
or
\dim(Z)=\dim(Y)
, it suffices, by Lemma
4.7
(
4
), to prove the case when
X
and
Y
are irreducible. Under this assumption, if
\dim(X)=\dim(Y)
or if
\dim(Z)=\dim(Y)
, then any rational multi-section
U
for
Z\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>Y
is a rational multi-section for
Z\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X
and vice versa. In particular,

\RD(U\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>Y)\leq\RD(U\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X)

and taking the minimum over dense subsets of such, we see that
\RD(Z\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>Y)\leq\RD(Z\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X)
. The equality

\RD(Z\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X)=\max\{\RD(Z\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>Y),\RD(Y\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>X)\}

follows from what we have shown above.
∎

Special cases of the following are implicit in
[
Se45
,
Br45
,
